Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any homologies module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 413. The projective resolution has length at most 770 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_413(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 413. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^770(S, F) is trivial. QED.
